# Ted Lasso S01E08 — Pub Dart Sequence Visual Evidence v0.1

## A. Source and applicability audit

- Evidence ID: `TED-LASSO-S01E08-DARTS-REVERSAL-001`
- Work / season / episode: *Ted Lasso*, S01E08
- Identity boundary: `TITLE_AND_EPISODE_FILENAME_SUPPLIED`; the pub/dartboard/exterior target sequence is visually corroborated, while exact title/season/episode identity is not picture-proven
- Local source filename: local S01E08 MP4; raw downloader label and media are not reproduced
- Video track: H.264, `1920x1080`, `24000/1001 fps` (approximately `23.976 fps`), `42761 frames / 1783.490s`
- Container duration: approximately `1783.520s`
- Container audio: AAC, `2ch/48 kHz`; audible content was not directly auditioned
- Continuous scene: NO — this is one contiguous selected edit envelope with a long visibly distinct riverside block; `CONTINUOUS_DRAMATIC_SEQUENCE_WITH_PARALLEL_INSERTS` is an INFERRED research classification, not proof of simultaneity or uninterrupted story time
- Natural single-space scene: NO — pub material (`S001–S060`) hard-cuts to a riverside block (`S061–S090`) and returns to pub material (`S091–S147`)
- Original dialogue and ambience preserved: UNKNOWN
- Selected analysis envelope: `F27915 / 00:19:24.288` inclusive to `F37655 / 00:26:10.527` end-exclusive
- Frame-exact duration: `9740 frames / 406.239167s`
- Why this sequence tests the selected scene problem: The picture can test how a public room, a circular segmented wall board, two central people, multiple observer layers, small-object inserts, a long distinct-location interruption, an anchor-first return, extended single-person ownership, and later distributed group/contact states are ordered. Picture alone cannot prove a game, wager, rules, underestimation, authority, skill, score, biography, victory, reversal, status change, shared reaction cause, dialogue or sound.
- Suitability: PARTIAL — strong for visible room/target/person sequencing, exact edit rhythm, the distinct-location interruption and return, object-state inserts, sustained screen ownership and distributed body/contact states; semantic trigger, object identity across cuts, cause/result and all sound remain script-gated, INFERRED or UNKNOWN.

### Selected-envelope and boundary audit

- OBSERVED: `F27914 / 00:19:24.246` is the last frame of a different auditorium/press-room view; `F27915 / 00:19:24.288` hard-cuts to a pub group view containing `P-G`, `P-E`, `P-F` and drinking vessels.
- OBSERVED: `S001–S060 / 00:19:24.288–00:21:57.691` remain in a pub with a circular segmented wall board, central standing people, seated/table groups, window/door regions and repeated drinking vessels.
- OBSERVED: `F31593 / 00:21:57.691` hard-cuts to a visibly distinct riverside exterior. `S061–S090` contain `P-X`, `P-Y`, `P-Z`, path/river/bridge/steps geometry, a camera-like device, a small rectangular surface and visible person-to-person contact.
- OBSERVED: `F33635 / 00:23:22.860` hard-cuts from a rear exterior walking view to a close view of the circular segmented board; `S092` immediately widens to the pub group.
- OBSERVED: `S113 / 00:24:17.998–00:24:32.388` is the longest accepted shot and keeps `P-A` in a crowd-backed single; further `P-A` singles recur around board and selected-person views through `S128`.
- OBSERVED: `S128 / 00:25:30.988–00:25:31.530` isolates the board center with a slim dart-like object visible there. `S129–S147` then distribute table, individual, pair/contact and group states.
- OBSERVED: `F37654 / 00:26:10.486` is the final pub frame; `F37655 / 00:26:10.527` hard-cuts to a visibly different outdoor training-field wide.
- INFERRED: The recurring pub people/board and return order support treating the full range as one selected dramatic sequence with a distinct-location insert. This does not prove simultaneity, causality or dialogue continuity.
- BOUNDARY: Automated cut scores and derived contact sheets were navigation aids. The table records manually accepted picture discontinuities; subtitle changes, fast hands, faces, vessels, dart-like motion, exposure changes and overlay changes are not cuts by default.

## B. Scene dramatic structure

- Start state — OBSERVED (`S001–S017 / 00:19:24.288–00:19:54.568`): Pub people occupy standing, seated and board-side zones; vessels recur; `S016` shows a hand with a slim dart-like object extended toward the board-side direction and `S017` shows a board/object state.
- Start-state interpretation — UNKNOWN: A toast, match, challenge, turn, rules, target, competence and social hierarchy cannot be established from picture alone.
- Objectives — UNKNOWN (`S001–S147`): Names, roles, relationships, spoken goals, contest goals and private goals are not visually established.
- Obstacle — UNKNOWN (`S001–S147`): Underestimation, arrogance, pressure, stakes, wager and hidden skill require a locked script and are excluded from `OBSERVED`.
- Visible event chain — OBSERVED (`S035–S060`, `S091–S128`): Central people, board views, an open case of slim objects, marked-surface inserts, extended `P-A` singles and a board-center object state are interleaved.
- Event interpretation — INFERRED/UNKNOWN: This order may support an object-mediated public contest and competence reversal when a locked script supplies those facts. Picture alone cannot assign thrower, score, order, legality, success, defeat or winner.
- Distinct-location turn — OBSERVED (`S060→S061`, `S061–S090`, `S090→S091`): The edit leaves the pub for thirty riverside shots and returns first to the circular-board close-up, then to the pub group.
- Distinct-location interpretation — INFERRED: It may be a parallel narrative insert that delays the pub sequence's visible next state. Simultaneity, cause, elapsed story time and connection are UNKNOWN.
- Reaction points — UNKNOWN as drama (`S092–S147`): faces, mouths, gazes, raised hands/vessels and contact states are visible, but no shared stimulus or reaction cause is picture-proven.
- End state — OBSERVED (`S129–S147 / 00:25:31.530–00:26:10.527`): the edit moves among a table group, `P-H`, `P-C`, `P-A`, `P-B/P-D`, two contact states and a final table/group vessel extension before leaving the pub.
- End-state interpretation — UNKNOWN: Victory, loss, humiliation, admiration, reconciliation, celebration, status change and future consequences are not visually proven.
- POV — INFERRED: objective/public-room and selected-person/object aligned rather than verified optical character POV; long `P-A` ownership changes screen attention but does not prove audience identification.
- Audience information — INFERRED: the audience can track recurring pub regions, selected people and visible board/object states, plus a separate exterior block; knowledge states and the meaning of those images remain script-gated.

## D. Coverage, spatial design, blocking and continuity ledgers

- OBSERVED (`S001–S017 / 00:19:24.288–00:19:54.568`): the envelope opens after the pub is already occupied. Table, central standing and board/window regions appear through group/pair/single coverage; no principal person's complete ingress is covered.
- OBSERVED (`S018–S060 / 00:19:54.568–00:21:57.691`): recurring `P-A/P-B`, `P-C`, `P-D`, table group and board/object views preserve recognizable screen regions, but exact camera stations, floor measurements and focal lengths are not established.
- INFERRED: repeated facing directions support a workable central pair axis between `P-A/P-B`, with board and witness layers on adjacent planes. The intended 180-degree line, any deliberate axis crossing and who owns the axis remain UNKNOWN.
- OBSERVED (`S061–S090 / 00:21:57.691–00:23:22.860`): the edit changes to path/river/bridge geometry, alternates walking/relation/single/contact views, introduces `P-Z` at the steps in `S078`, and ends on `P-X/P-Y` walking away. Their complete arrival and final departure from the exterior are not shown.
- OBSERVED (`S091–S112 / 00:23:22.860–00:24:17.998`): return order is board close-up → pub group → marked surface/person/group coverage. This restores object and room layers without proving the exterior interval's narrative relation.

- UNKNOWN: complete pub ingress/egress, exact entrances/exits outside the selected envelope, hidden blocking, floor plan, lens/support, optical POV, intended axis, object flight, cross-cut object custody and safety/rehearsal method.

## E. Editing and rhythm

- Visible-shot count: `147`.
- Exact frame coverage: `9740 frames / 406.239167s`.
- Mean: `2.763532s`; median: `1.918583s`; inclusive Q1: `1.292958s`; inclusive Q3: `3.440937s` (the exact midpoint is `3.4409375s`, reported at the accepted six-decimal convention).
- Minimum: `0.458792s` (`S017`); maximum: `14.389375s` (`S113`).
- Distribution: `<1s`: `22`; `1–<2s`: `53`; `2–<4s`: `43`; `4–<8s`: `20`; `>=8s`: `9`.
- Phase mapping: P0 `S001–S017`; P1 `S018–S060`; P2 `S061–S090`; P3 `S091–S112`; P4 `S113–S128`; P5 `S129–S147`.

- OBSERVED: The pub opening uses rapid group/pair/single alternation and brief object inserts; the longest early holds are `S024` (`8.049708s`), `S026` (`8.883875s`) and `S029` (`10.176833s`).
- OBSERVED: The distinct-location block contains `30 shots / 85.168417s`; it alternates walking pair, close opposing singles, a visible contact, camera-like-device/third-person states and a rear departure.
- OBSERVED: Pub return begins with `S091` board (`0.709042s`) then `S092` group (`1.126125s`), followed by marked-surface, person and group views.
- OBSERVED: `P4` includes extended `P-A` ownership in `S113` (`14.389375s`), `S117` (`8.675333s`), `S121` (`13.096417s`) and `S125` (`9.426083s`), interleaved with other people and board states.
- OBSERVED: After `S128` board-center close-up (`0.542208s`), `S129–S147` move through brief group/person views and longer pair/contact/group views; durations alone do not prove a result or reaction.
- INFERRED: Anchor-first return and later selected group/contact distribution may help reorient and then allocate screen ownership when a locked script supplies a public contest/result. This is conditional, not a universal cadence.
- UNKNOWN: Hidden/sub-frame edits, production takes, focal-length progression, exact camera support, dialogue timing, pauses, sound-led cuts, score/impact cues and intentionality.

## F. Sound and performance grammar

- All `147` rows are `SND-UNK`: the audio stream exists but was not directly auditioned. Dialogue, speaker attribution, pub/exterior ambience, score, silence, overlap, cheers, gasps, glass sounds, dart/impact sounds, off-screen cues and sound bridges remain UNKNOWN.
- Burned subtitle overlays were excluded. They were not transcribed and do not prove names, relationships, wager, rules, underestimation, competence, biography, score, victory, defeat, reconciliation, reaction cause or edit motivation.
- OBSERVED: walking, face/head/gaze/mouth changes, hand/dart-like/case/slate/vessel states, crowd posture, face/body contact and room/exterior region changes occur across accepted picture units.
- INFERRED: Repeated `P-A` singles change visible screen ownership; board/object inserts and selected later people distribute picture information. Neither pattern identifies dialogue or emotion without a locked script.
- UNKNOWN: speech/listening, interruption, silence, comic timing, threat, flirtation, consent, applause, vocal escalation, shared stimulus and performance intent.

## H. Evidence boundary

### OBSERVED

- The selected `F27915–F37655` envelope, `147` manually accepted visible picture units and exact timecodes in the table.
- Pub people/zones, circular-board and slim-object appearances, open case, marked-surface appearances, repeated vessels, the riverside interruption, camera-like-device and small-rectangle appearances, visible person-to-person contact, board-first pub return, long `P-A` singles, board-center state and later distributed person/group/contact states.
- The existence and metadata of video/audio streams; audible content was not verified.

### INFERRED

- `CONTINUOUS_DRAMATIC_SEQUENCE_WITH_PARALLEL_INSERTS` as a research classification.
- Objective/public-room editorial POV, cut motivation, anchor-first reorientation, screen-ownership shifts and the four model-neutral transfer proposals.
- Any claim that the exterior block delays the pub event, or that the later group/person sequence is a receipt cascade, remains conditional on a locked script.

### HYPOTHESIS / CANDIDATE

- A public object contest may benefit from pre-result geometry, anchor-first return after a genuine thread break, separate action/target/result states and selective post-result body-state distribution.
- None is a default or production rule until independent evidence, same-trigger counterevidence, an original locked-script forward test and human creative review exist.

### UNKNOWN

- Dialogue and every audible fact; exact identities/roles/relationships; game/wager/rules; underestimation; authority; competence; biography; score; thrower/order/impact; winner/loser; reversal/status change; shared stimulus; reaction causes; object provenance/identity across cuts; simultaneity/causality/story time; exact axis/lens/support/focus method; hidden joins; production intent.

### Rights-safe transfer boundary

- Do not copy the source's people, costumes, pub, dartboard design, branded/marked surfaces, props, dialogue, joke, exterior incident, shot composition, blocking event, performance or narrative reversal into original generation instructions.
- Transfer only abstract decision logic with project-original people, geometry, target/object design, witness layout, body/contact states, timing and assets.
- No source video, audio, frame, still, contact sheet, subtitle, transcript, copyrighted dialogue, raw downloader label or private production material is included in this evidence package.
